# pageListItemBlocks

**Description** : *List item*

**Layer** : 233

```tl
pageListItemBlocks#63ca67aa flags:# checkbox:flags.0?true checked:flags.1?true blocks:Vector<PageBlock> = PageListItem;
```

---

## Parameters

| Name | Type | Description |
| :---: | :---: | :--- |
| <del style = 'opacity : 0.4'>flags</del> | [`#`](type/#) | NOTHING |
| **checkbox** | [`flags.0?true`](type/true) | NOTHING |
| **checked** | [`flags.1?true`](type/true) | NOTHING |
| <mark>blocks</mark> | [`Vector<PageBlock>`](type/PageBlock) | Blocks |

---

## Type

[PageListItem](type/PageListItem)
